```
class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int) # net out degree 
        for x, y in pairs: 
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1
                
        for k in degree: 
            if degree[k] = 1: 
                x = k
                break 
                
        ans = []

        def fn(x): 
            """Return Eulerian path via dfs."
            while graph[x]: fn(graph[x].pop()) 
            ans.append(x)
        
        dfn(s)
        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i+1]] for i in range(len(ans)-1)]
```

Let's think of Eulerian path algorithm.

Given directed graph edges pairs. Need find path using all edges exactly once. Hierholzer:
- Build adjacency list.
- Compute out-in degree.
- Find start:
  - If any node with out-in == 1, start = that node.

```
    u = stack[-1]

        v = graph[u].pop()
        stack.append(v)
    else:
        path.append(stack.pop())
path.reverse()
```

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int)
        for x, y in pairs:
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1

        start = None
        for k in degree:
            if degree[k] == 1:
                start = k
                break
        if start is None:
            start = next(iter(graph))

        ans = []

        def fn(x):
            while graph[x]:
                fn(graph[x].pop())
            ans.append(x)

        fn(start)
        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i+1]] for i in range(len(ans)-1)]
```

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int)

        for x, y in pairs:
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1

        start = None
        for node, d in degree.items():
            if d == 1:
                start = node
                break

        if start is None:
            start = next(iter(graph), None)

        if start is None:
            return []

        ans = []
        stack = [start]

        while stack:
            u = stack[-1]
            if graph[u]:
                v = graph[u].pop()
                stack.append(v)
            else:
                ans.append(stack.pop())

        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i+1]] for i in range(len(ans)-1)]
```

Need check `next(iter(graph), None)` works with defaultdict? `iter(graph)` returns iterator over keys. `next(iter(graph), None)` okay. If graph empty, returns None. But if graph empty and pairs empty, return []. Good.

Potential issue: `if graph[u]:` with `graph` defaultdict will insert `u` if not present. This can happen when stack contains a node that has no outgoing edges and was not in graph (e.g., target-only node). It inserts empty list. That's okay. But if `start` is None and graph empty, return. If graph non-empty, start exists. Good.

Potential issue: If `start = next(iter(graph), None)` and graph is defaultdict with keys but all values empty? Not possible because keys are source nodes with outgoing edges. But if we inserted a node as key via `graph[u]` in the while loop? That happens after start selection. So okay.

Potential issue: If graph has nodes with outgoing edges but degree dict has no node with d==1, start any. Good.

Potential issue: If graph has node with d==1 but start is None? no.

Potential issue: If graph has Eulerian path and start selected by d==1, but graph[start] may have multiple outgoing edges. Good.

Potential issue: If graph has Eulerian path but no d==1, start any. Good.

Potential issue: If graph has Eulerian path but start selected by d==1, but there is also a node with d==1? impossible. If invalid, maybe choose first. Fine.

Need maybe if `degree` is defaultdict(int), iterating `degree.items()` while not modifying degree? We don't modify during iteration. Good.

Need maybe if `graph[u]` in while loop and graph is defaultdict, if u is a node with no outgoing but not in graph, it inserts u. This could cause `graph` to grow, but no issue. However, if `u` is a node with no outgoing and not in graph, `graph[u]` returns [] and inserts. Then `ans.append(stack.pop())`. Fine.

Need maybe if `graph[u]` is a list and we pop from it, if it becomes empty, next time `if graph[u]` false. Good.

Need maybe if there are duplicate edges, pop removes one. Good.

Now, should we include `#` comments? The final code can be:

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int)

        for x, y in pairs:
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1

        start = None
        for node, d in degree.items():
            if d == 1:
                start = node
                break

        if start is None:
            start = next(iter(graph), None)

        if start is None:
            return []

        ans = []
        stack = [start]

        while stack:
            u = stack[-1]
            if graph[u]:
                stack.append(graph[u].pop())
            else:
                ans.append(stack.pop())

        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i+1]] for i in range(len(ans) - 1)]
```

```
while stack:
    u = stack[-1]
    if not graph[u]:
        ans.append(stack.pop())
    else:
        stack.append(graph[u].pop())
```
This is equivalent. Our code:
```
if graph[u]:
    stack.append(graph[u].pop())
else:
    ans.append(stack.pop())
```

</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int)

        for x, y in pairs:
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1

        start = None
        for node, d in degree.items():
            if d == 1:
                start = node
                break

        if start is None:
            start = next(iter(graph), None)

        if start is None:
            return []

        ans = []
        stack = [start]

        while stack:
            u = stack[-1]
            if graph[u]:
                stack.append(graph[u].pop())
            else:
                ans.append(stack.pop())

        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i + 1]] for i in range(len(ans) - 1)]
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def validArrangement(self, pairs: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        degree = defaultdict(int)

        for x, y in pairs:
            graph[x].append(y)
            degree[x] += 1
            degree[y] -= 1

        start = None
        for node, d in degree.items():
            if d == 1:
                start = node
                break

        if start is None:
            start = next(iter(graph), None)

        if start is None:
            return []

        ans = []
        stack = [start]

        while stack:
            u = stack[-1]
            if graph[u]:
                stack.append(graph[u].pop())
            else:
                ans.append(stack.pop())

        ans.reverse()
        return [[ans[i], ans[i + 1]] for i in range(len(ans) - 1)]
